 1. Describe a time when you helped someone
You should say:
 - Where you helped him/her
 - Why you helped
 - How you helped
 - And explain how you felt about it

 ¥ Man is a social animal.
 ¥ Giving and receiving help is part of living in society.
 ¥ One memorable instance where I offered assistance to someone in need was when I helped a
 blind person cross the road.
 ¥ It happened about 2 months ago.
 ¥ As I was waiting at a busy intersecEon, I noticed a visually impaired gentleman standing on
 the corner.
 ¥ It looked as if he wanted to cross the road, but was not sure, as there was too much traffic
 noise around him.
 ¥ I approached him and asked if he needed help crossing the road.
 ¥ The gentleman, Mr. Singh, was grateful for the assistance.
 ¥ He explained that he needed to reach the other side, but the traffic noise was making it
 challenging for him.
 ¥ Taking his arm to guide him, I waited for a suitable gap in the traffic.
 ¥ Upon safely reaching the other side, Mr. Singh expressed his gratitude, thanking me for the
 assistance.
 ¥ He shared a bit about his daily challenges in geKng around independently, highlighEng the
 importance of simple acts of kindness from strangers.
 ¥ Helping Mr. Singh cross the road leL me with a profound sense of fulfilment.
 ¥ I realised that the small acts of kindness can have a profound impact on someone's day,
 especially when faced with challenges.
 ¥ It made me reflect on the importance of empathy and the significance of extending a helping
 hand to those in need.
 ¥ I felt a deep satisfaction in knowing that I had made a tangible difference in someone's life,
 even if just for a brief moment.
 ¥ Lending a helping hand, no maRer how small the gesture, can create a ripple effect of
 posiEvity in the world.

Or

¥ Man is a social animal.
¥ We all have to give and receive help from others many times.
¥ Here I am going to talk about a situaEon when I helped an accident vicEm.
¥ It so happened that I was going to Ludhiana last month to attend a wedding.
¥ On the way there is a petrol pump.
¥ We saw that a lady was lying on the side and there was a lot of blood from her head.
¥ This accident had just happened 2 minutes before.
¥ She and her husband were on a scooter and as they were coming out of the petrol pump a
 speeding car hit them and sped away.

¥ We parked our car on the side and came out to see if we could help.
¥ Her husband had minor injuries but she was in a bad shape.
¥ We immediately called 108 for help.
¥ It is the number of the free ambulance run by the government.
¥ Within fiLeen minutes the ambulance was there.
¥ We did not move the lady because it could aggravate her injury.
¥ She was breathing properly but had a lot of pain.
¥ There was a first aid team headed by a doctor in the ambulance.
¥ The ambulance people picked her very carefully on a stretcher and immediately rushed her to
 Phillaur Civil Hospital.
¥ We took the number of other relaEves of the paEent from her husband and called them.
¥ Actually he was also very shocked because of his wife's condiEon.
¥ We took him to the Phillaur hospital in our car.
¥ Only aLer they were seRled, we moved on.
¥ We were late for the wedding, but we were happy that we could help some needy people.

Part 3 Follow-up Questions

Should children be taught to be kind to others?
The quality of being kind to others should definitely be inculcated in children. This will lead to a society
with less suffering and more happiness. Moreover, children should be taught other values as well,
such as being honest and respecZul.

Should people be very kind when they help others?
Yes, I believe one should be very polite and humble while helping someone in distress or need.
Rude behaviour during such a time would simply negate the kindness of the act. Also, one should
not try to make it look like they are doing a favour to someone and they expect the same in return.

Can kids provide any help to parents?
Kids can assist their parents in mulEple ways. This will also help them to learn new skills. They can do
many chores around the house; for example, children can help parents with cleaning the house,
cooking, washing dishes, laundry, or taking the dog for a walk.

Should parents give children advice? Why?
Definitely yes, and the simple reason behind this is that young minds are too fragile to disEnguish
between right and wrong. They need constant guidance from their elders' experience to make the
right decisions. If the parents are not there to guide the young, they can easily go astray.

What kind of advice should parents give to their children?
I believe that children mostly imitate the way their parents behave, so the only advice that parents
need to give their children is to always do the right thing. Parents should reinforce this advice by
leading by example rather than expecEng their children to behave well on their own.

Should parents help their kids with their homework?
Well, the homework is normally assigned to the children and not the parents, so I don't think that
parents should help them in doing it. However, they should definitely monitor whether their children
are compleEng their homework on time or not. Finally, in excepEonal cases, when children are finding
an assignment to be tough, they can ask for their parents' help.

 2. Describe a book you enjoyed reading and that made
 you happy
You should say:
 - What kind of book it was
 - What it was about
 - Why you read it
 - And explain why it made you happy

 ¥ I've read many books over the years, but the very first one I ever read is sEll one of my
 favorites.
 ¥ It's Harry PoRer and the Philosopher's Stone, wriRen by J.K. Rowling.
 ¥ I read it when I was about 10 years old and it was actually a birthday giL from my mom.
 ¥ At the time, I was a liRle upset with her because I had really wanted a new video game that
 had just been released.
 ¥ My mom promised me that she'd get me the game too if I gave the book a chance first.
 ¥ So, rather reluctantly, I started reading it.
 ¥ To my surprise, aLer just 10 pages, I completely hooked.
 ¥ I started reading it at 9 PM that night and couldn't stop.
 ¥ I finally finished it at 7 AM the next morning.
 ¥ Luckily, it was summer vacaEon, or my parents would've taken the book away and made me
 go to sleep.
 ¥ The story, as many people know, follows a young boy named Harry who discovers on his 11th
 birthday that he is a wizard.
 ¥ He goes to Hogwarts School of WitchcraL and Wizardry and begins a magical journey filled
 with friendship, danger, and a struggle between good and evil.

Part 3 Follow Up Questions

How can parents help children develop a love of reading?
I think parents can do a lot to help children develop a love of reading. They should make a variety of
books easily available at home and allow their children to choose books that genuinely interest them.
Parents can also read with their children or tell them stories from a young age, which can make
reading feel like an enjoyable activity rather than a chore. Finally, for younger children, parents can
choose books with colorful illustrations and engaging stories to make reading more appealing.

Why do some people prefer watching movies to reading books?
I think some people prefer watching movies because they're visually engaging and much faster to
consume than books. Although books often provide more detail and insight into the characters'
thoughts, reading requires more time and concentration. For example, the Harry Potter books go
much deeper into the story than the movies, but watching a movie only takes a couple of hours. So,
for people who are busy or simply want to relax, movies can be a more convenient form of
entertainment.

Do you think people today read less than before? Why
Yes, I think people read less these days because there are so many other forms of entertainment
available. For example, people spend more time watching TV because of streaming services or using
social media. In addition, many people have demanding jobs and simply don't have enough time to
sit down and read a book.

Are success books useful to everyone?
I don't think success books are useful to everyone. Everyone has different goals and circumstances,
so the advice in such books may not work for everyone. On the other hand, some may find them
useful and get motivated by reading such books.

Do you think people should read the original book before watching the movie?
No, not really. I think some movie or TV adaptations are excellent even if you haven't read the original
book. For example, a lot of my friends haven't read the Game of Thrones series, but they still really
enjoyed the TV adaptation. Ultimately, I think it comes down to personal preference and how much
time someone has. Reading an entire series can take weeks or even months, whereas watching the
adaptation is often much more convenient.

What kinds of books are most popular in India?
I think many kinds of books are popular in India because it is such a diverse country, and people have
diverse tastes in everything, including reading. For example, some people enjoy fiction, such as
romance, thrillers, and fantasy, while others prefer biographies, history, or self-help books. Religious
and spiritual books are also quite popular

 and a healthy daily routine

¥ With today's fast-paced lifestyle and busy schedules, it is becoming increasingly difficult to
 develop healthy habits and maintain a healthy routine.
¥ However, I know one person who has managed to do exactly that.
¥ He is my maternal uncle, and he is a brigadier in the army.
¥ He joined the army at a very young age, even before he turned 18.
¥ My mother grew up in a joint family, so she is very close to all her cousins.
¥ He used to visit us quite often when I was younger, but as he became busier with his career, we
 started seeing him less frequently.
¥ He developed many healthy habits after joining the army.
¥ In fact, my mother remembers how difficult it was for him in the beginning.
¥ He used to call home every day and say that he wanted to leave the army.
¥ However, eventually, he adjusted to the lifestyle and became extremely disciplined.
¥ He gets up at 4 o'clock every morning and then runs for around 10 kilometres.

¥ He said he could enjoy all those things after retirement because they were not going anywhere.

 India.
¥ He says that if he has to be somewhere at 9 a.m., he will be there by 9 at the latest.
¥ He may arrive early, but I have never seen him arrive late.
¥ I really admire his discipline and consistency.
¥ He has shown me that maintaining a healthy lifestyle is not just about exercising or eating well; it
 is also about having self-control and sticking to a routine.
¥ I hope I can develop the same kind of discipline and become more like him as I grow older.

Part 3 Follow Up Questions

1. Does the government play an important role in promoting healthy lifestyles in a community?
Yes, I believe the government plays a significant role in promoting healthy lifestyles. It can launch
awareness campaigns about the importance of exercise, a balanced diet and regular health check-
ups. It can also provide facilities such as public parks, walking tracks and sports centres so that people
have convenient places to exercise. In addition, the government can regulate unhealthy products,
such as sugary drinks and tobacco, through taxes and advertising restrictions. However, individuals
also need to take responsibility for their own health.

2. Do you think it is important to live in a community where people have healthy lifestyles?
Yes, definitely. People are strongly influenced by those around them, so living in a health-conscious
community can encourage individuals to adopt better habits. For example, if people regularly go for
walks, exercise together or eat healthy food, others may be more likely to do the same. It can also
create a supportive environment where people motivate each other to stay fit. On the other hand, if
unhealthy habits are common in a community, people may gradually adopt them as well.

3. How can people relieve stress in daily life?
There are several ways people can manage stress in their daily lives. First of all, regular physical
activity can be very effective because it helps people relax and improves their mood. People can also
spend time doing activities they enjoy, such as reading, listening to music or meeting friends and
family. Another important thing is getting enough sleep and taking short breaks from work or studies.
Personally, I think having a proper routine and not taking on more responsibilities than one can
handle can prevent stress from building up.

4. What effect does stress have on health?
Stress can have both short-term and long-term effects on health. In the short term, it can cause
problems such as headaches, tiredness and difficulty concentrating. If a person experiences stress for
a long period, it can also affect their sleep and overall well-being. In some cases, chronic stress can
contribute to more serious health problems. Therefore, it is important to recognise the signs of stress
and find healthy ways to manage it.

5. What makes people stressed?
I think people experience stress for a variety of reasons, but work and financial problems are probably
two of the most common ones. Many people have demanding jobs and struggle to maintain a balance
between their professional and personal lives. Students, on the other hand, may feel stressed
because of exams, academic pressure or expectations from their parents. Relationship problems can
also be a major source of stress. In general, people tend to become stressed when they feel that they
have too many responsibilities and not enough time or resources to deal with them.

6. Which group pays more attention to health, young people or old people?
I think older people generally tend to pay more attention to their health, although this is changing
among younger generations. As people get older, they become more aware of the importance of
regular exercise, a healthy diet and medical check-ups. Young people often take their health for
granted because they usually have more energy and fewer health concerns. However, social media
and greater awareness of fitness have encouraged many young people to exercise and eat more
healthily. So, I would say older people are generally more health-conscious, but the difference is
becoming smaller.

 4. Describe a way/change that helps you save a lot of
 time

 - What it is
 - How you implement it
 - How difficult it is
 - And explain how you feel about the way/change

¥ One change I made recently that has helped me save a considerable amount of time is limiting
 my social media usage to just one hour a day.
¥ I used to spend quite a lot of time scrolling through Instagram, especially when I had a short break
 or when I was feeling bored.
¥ The problem was that I would often open the app just for a few minutes, but end up spending
 much longer than I had planned.
¥ I realised that it was taking away a lot of time that I could have spent on more productive things.
¥ So, I decided to put a one-hour limit on my social media usage.
¥ Since I use an iPhone, I used the Screen Time feature to set a one-hour daily limit for Instagram.
¥ Once I have used Instagram for an hour, my phone blocks the app and reminds me that I have
 reached my limit.
¥ I can technically ignore the warning, but I try not to.
¥ Initially, it was quite difficult because I was so used to checking Instagram whenever I had some
 free time.
¥ I would sometimes automatically reach for my phone without even having a particular reason to
 use it.
¥ However, after a few days, I gradually got used to the restriction.
¥ The biggest benefit is that I now have much more free time.
¥ Instead of spending an hour or two mindlessly scrolling, I can use that time to work, read, exercise
 or simply relax.
¥ I also feel that I am more in control of my time because I am consciously deciding how to spend it
 rather than letting social media consume it.
¥ Overall, I think it was a very small change, but it has made a surprisingly big difference to my daily
 routine.
¥ I don't think I need to completely give up social media; I just need to make sure that I am using it
 rather than letting it use me.

Part 3 Follow-up Questions

1. What can schools do to help students learn time management?
Schools can take a number of initiatives to make students more conscious of time. Firstly, they could
reward punctuality by giving awards to students who have never been late throughout the school
year. On the other hand, they could also penalise students who are consistently late. Teachers can
also encourage children to maintain journals or timetables so that they learn how to organise their
daily activities and complete tasks on time.

2. Is it necessary for children to learn time management?
Yes, absolutely. Time management is the key to success in almost every field. I think punctuality and
the ability to manage time are particularly important for children because these habits can stay with
them throughout their lives. If they learn to prioritise tasks and meet deadlines at a young age, they
are likely to find it much easier to manage their studies and eventually their professional
responsibilities.

3. Do people who can manage time well become successful more easily?
I think so, because good time management allows people to make better use of the limited time they
have. For example, someone who plans their day properly can finish their important tasks without
wasting time on unnecessary activities. This can make them more productive and help them achieve
their goals faster. However, time management alone does not guarantee success, as skills,
opportunities and hard work are also important.

4. Do you think parents should be responsible for teaching children to save time?
Yes, to some extent. Parents are usually the first people who can teach children basic habits such as
being punctual, following a routine and finishing their work on time. For instance, they can encourage
their children to get ready for school without constantly reminding them or to complete their
homework before playing. However, I think schools should reinforce these habits as well, so the
responsibility should be shared.

5. Does technology help people save time? How and why?
Yes, definitely. Technology has made many everyday activities much quicker and more convenient.
For example, people can pay bills, transfer money and shop online within a few minutes, whereas
these activities used to require a physical visit. Similarly, navigation apps help people find the fastest
route and avoid traffic. However, technology can also waste time if people become addicted to social
media or spend too much time on their phones.

6. What can people do to save time?
I think the most effective thing is to plan their day in advance and prioritise important tasks. People
should make a realistic schedule and avoid spending too much time on activities that are not
particularly useful. They can also use technology, such as calendar and reminder apps, to organise
their work. Another important thing is to avoid multitasking, because constantly switching between
different tasks can actually reduce productivity.

Part 3 Follow Up Questions

What do people often feel after losing things?
After losing things, people often feel upset, frustrated, or worried, especially if they lose something
important. They may get stressed and anxious while searching for it.

What can we do to reduce the possibility of losing things?
To reduce the chance of losing things, we should try to keep things organized. We should have
designated places for things, and we should always place them there.
Being mindful, alert, and double-checking things before leaving a place also helps.

What kinds of people may lose things often?
People who are forgetful, disorganized, or always in a hurry may lose things often. Absent-minded
people and people who have too many things on their minds also misplace items easily.

Is it effective to offer rewards for finding lost things?
Yes, offering rewards can help find lost things. It motivates people to look for an item and return it
if they find it.

What will people do when losing things?
When people lose things, they usually search places they last visited, ask others for help, and try to
remember where they had it last. If it is an important item, they may file a police report, offer a
reward, and post notices.

What kinds of things do people usually lose?
People usually lose small and everyday items like keys, wallets, watches, glasses, phones, jewelry,
etc.

Part 3 Follow Up Questions
1. What should we not talk about when we meet a person for the first time?
When meeting someone for the first time, it's best to avoid talking about sensitive topics like
politics, religion, or personal finances. These topics can be uncomfortable and may lead to
disagreements. It's better to stick to light and neutral subjects until you get to know the person
better.

2. What can we do to leave a good impression on people we meet for the first time?
To leave a good impression, I think it's important to be polite, listen attentively, and show genuine
interest in the other person. Being positive and respectful makes people feel comfortable around
you, and that can lead to a lasting good impression.

3. What qualities make true friends?
I believe that true friends are those who are trustworthy, supportive, and understanding. They are
always there for you in both good times and bad, and they respect your feelings and opinions.
Loyalty and honesty are qualities that I value most in a true friend.

4. Is it important to have the same hobbies and interests when making friends?
It's not essential to have the same hobbies, but sharing common interests can help build a
connection. I think that what matters more is mutual respect and understanding. Differences in
hobbies can make friendships more interesting and provide opportunities to learn from each other.

5. On what occasions do people like to make friends?
People often make friends in social situations such as school, work, or at events like parties or
gatherings. It's easier to make friends when you are in environments where you can interact with
others and share experiences.

6. How do people make friends in India?
In India, people usually make friends through school, college, work, or even through family
connections. Social gatherings and community events also play a big role in meeting new people. I
think people in India tend to build strong relationships through shared cultural and social activities.
Nowadays, people also make friends through social media.

Part 3 Follow Up Questions

1. What can we learn from history?
History teaches us about the mistakes and achievements of the past. By learning about history, we
can understand how societies have developed, avoid repeating past errors, and appreciate the
progress we've made. It also helps us learn about different cultures and traditions.

2. What do people need to remember in their daily lives?
In daily life, it's important to remember tasks, appointments, and responsibilities. We also need to
remember to take care of our health, maintain relationships, and stay organized. These things help
ensure we lead a balanced and successful life.

3. Can technology help people remember things better? How?
Yes, technology can help people remember things better by providing tools like reminders,
calendars, and apps designed to store and organize information. For example, phone apps can
remind us of tasks or appointments, making it easier to stay on track.

4. Which can help people remember things better, words or photos?
I think photos can sometimes be more effective than words when it comes to remembering things.
Visual memories tend to stick longer, and a photo can evoke emotions and memories in a way
words can't. However, words can be useful for detailed information or when we need to recall
specific facts.

5. Do people like things of memorial significance?
Yes, people often value things of memorial significance because they hold sentimental value. Items
like family heirlooms, photographs, or gifts can remind people of important moments and loved
ones, helping preserve memories for years to come.

6. Why do some people have a better memory than others?
Some people have a better memory due to a combination of genetics and lifestyle. Factors like
brain health, stress levels, and how actively a person engages in mental exercises can play a role. A
person who regularly challenges their brain with learning and puzzles might retain more
information.

Part 3 Follow Up Questions
 Are there any unlucky things that people should not buy as gifts in your country?
Yes, in some cultures, certain gifts are considered unlucky. For example, in my country, knives or
scissors are seen as symbols of cutting relationships, and clocks are associated with the idea of
running out of time. It's believed that gifting these items could bring bad luck or symbolize an end
to a relationship.

What do people usually consider when buying gifts?
When buying gifts, people usually consider the recipient's interests, preferences, and the occasion.
It's also important to think about the cultural significance of the gift, as some items might carry
special meanings. For instance, people might choose practical gifts or something that reflects the
recipient's personality.

 Which do people give on traditional festivals, red envelopes or other kinds of gifts?
In traditional festivals, particularly during Chinese New Year, red envelopes (hongbao) containing
money are commonly given. These envelopes symbolize good luck, prosperity, and well wishes.
Other gifts may include food, clothes, or items with symbolic meaning, but red envelopes are a
popular choice.

Will people feel happy when receiving an expensive gift?
It depends on the person and the relationship. Some people may appreciate the thought and
generosity behind an expensive gift, while others may feel uncomfortable or pressured by the cost.
Ultimately, it's the thought and personal connection that make the gift special, rather than its price
tag.

Is it difficult to choose a gift?
Choosing a gift can be challenging, especially if you're unsure about the recipient's preferences. It's
important to think about what they would appreciate and how the gift aligns with the occasion.
Sometimes, people choose practical gifts, but it's still tough to find something meaningful for every
individual.

When do people normally send gifts to others?
People typically send gifts on special occasions such as birthdays, weddings, holidays, and festivals.
Gifts are also exchanged to mark significant life events like graduations, promotions, or the birth of
a child. Additionally, gifts can be given as gestures of appreciation or gratitude at any time.

 doing it together.

 become healthier.
 ¥ So, actually I think we helped each other.

Part 3 Follow Up Questions
 1. What effect does stress have on health?
 Stress can hurt both physical and mental health. It can disturb our sleep and keep us up at
 night. As a result, a person may feel too tired to exercise and do their day-to-day activities.
 Stress can also dampen our mood and make us anxious and irritated. Eventually, it can lead
 to more serious health problems. So, it's important to relax and manage stress.

 2. Which group pays more attention to health, young people or old people?
 I think young people are paying more attention to their health these days. One reason is
 that they have information at their fingertips. They can easily find information about
 nutrition, exercise, and different fitness routines on the internet. They are influenced by
 fitness influencers and celebrities on social media. So, they are more health conscious and
 want to look good and stay fit.

 Do you think people who live in natural places are more likely to protect them?
 Yes, people who live close to nature can understand its importance better because they see it
 every day. They may also depend on it for their work and daily life. So, they are more likely to
 protect it from pollution and damage.

 Do you think it is necessary to include natural spaces in a city?
 Yes, I think every city should have parks, trees, and other green spaces. It gives people a place
 to relax and spend time with their families and friends. These spaces make cities more attractive
 to live in.

 Are there any natural attractions that are great to visit?
 I think many natural attractions are worth visiting, like mountains, waterfalls, beaches, and
 national parks. These places allow people to relax and take a break from their daily routine.
 Visiting these places makes us more grateful for the beauty of nature and encourages us to
 preserve it for future generations.

1. What special events are celebrated in your country?
 India is a diverse country, so we celebrate a wide range of events throughout the year. Festivals
 like Diwali, Holi, Rakhi, Navaratri, and Onam are celebrated with a lot of excitement. People
 also celebrate national events like Republic Day and Independence Day. These celebrations
 involve traditional food, dances, and cultural activities.

2. Do most people prefer to hold a party in a restaurant or in a pub?
 Most people prefer restaurants for family celebrations like birthdays and anniversaries.
 Restaurants are more family-friendly and suitable for all ages. Many restaurants have separate
 rooms or bigger tables for large groups. Pubs are more popular among younger people for
 hanging out with friends.
3. Do most people prefer to hold a party in a public place or a private place?
 I think it depends on the occasion. For smaller celebrations, people often prefer private places
 like their homes because they feel more personal and comfortable. For larger events, such as
 weddings, people usually choose banquet halls, hotels, or other event venues.

4. When do people like to hold a party?
 People like to hold parties for special occasions like birthdays, anniversaries, weddings, and
 festivals. People also like to celebrate achievements like graduations, getting a new job, or
 buying a new home. Most celebrations are in the evening or on weekends.

Do you think old buildings are valuable?

 1. Can movies help people better understand the cultural background of a country?
 Yes, movies are an excellent way to learn more about a country's culture as they depict
 traditions, social values, lifestyles, food habits, and family relationships. They are a fun and
 engaging way to learn about another country. However, movies are not completely realistic,
 so people should also refer to other sources like books and magazines.

 2. Are movies more likely to help people become more creative than books?
 I think books encourage more creativity because readers have to imagine the characters,
 settings, and situations themselves. In movies, most details are already shown, so there is
 less left to our imagination.

 3. What are the differences between watching movies at home and in a cinema?
 Watching a movie at home is more convenient and affordable. You can pause, take breaks,
 and watch comfortably from your own couch. On the other hand, cinemas provide a more
 immersive experience because of the huge screen and surround sound system.

 4. What kinds of movies are the most popular in India?
 In India, movies that combine comedy, romance, action, and music are extremely popular.
 Lately, South Indian movies like Pushpa, RRR, and Bahubali have gained massive popularity.
 People also enjoy movies based on real-life events and inspiring figures, especially if they tell
 a powerful story.

 ¥ My mother first taught me how to cut the paper into pieces of the right size and then fold

 ¥ The basic technique wasn't very difficult, but learning how to arrange the pieces and create

 PART 3 - FOLLOW-UP QUESTIONS
1. Why is self-discipline important when learning a new skill?
 I think self-discipline is important because learning a new skill requires a lot of practice and
 patience. People can be motivated at first but lose motivation after a few days when things get
 difficult. So, people need to be self-disciplined to continue practicing even when they don't feel
 like it.

 2. What are the differences between learning from a teacher and learning by oneself?
 Learning with a teacher provides direct guidance, clear explanations, and immediate feedback,
 while learning by yourself requires more self-discipline, independent research, and critical thinking
 skills. It is also more convenient and flexible when people learn things themselves at their own
 pace.

3. How do adults learn a new skill?
 Adults can learn new skills in many ways. They can take in-person classes, watch online tutorials,
 read books, or learn from someone who already has experience in that area. Learning something
 new requires a lot of patience, self-discipline, and regular practice.

4. How can parents and teachers teach children new skills?
 I think teachers and parents should make learning fun and interactive for children. They should
 allow children to try the skill on their own. They also need to be patient and encouraging, as
 children are bound to make mistakes while learning.

 5. Where can children learn new skills?
 Children can learn new skills at school, as school offers many opportunities to explore different
 activities. They can also learn through extracurricular activities outside school, like music classes
 and sports academies. Nowadays, children can even learn skills at home through online tutorials
 and videos.
 6. Why should children learn new skills?
 I feel childhood is a great time to learn new skills because children can quickly pick up new skills
 and retain things better. They also have more time to practice. That is why children are
 encouraged to learn things like swimming, playing a musical instrument, or speaking a foreign
 language from a young age.

 history

 Who this person is
 How he/she learns history
 Why he/she loves history

 ¥ I would like to talk about my dad, who has become a real history buff over the past few
 years.
 ¥ What is interesting about him is that he was not always interested in history.
 ¥ When he was growing up, he found history very boring.
 ¥ He never enjoyed memorizing dates, names of rulers, or details about wars.
 ¥ He also had very little interest in visiting museums or historical places.
 ¥ If someone had suggested spending an entire day in a museum, he probably would have
 refused.
 ¥ However, his interests have completely changed over the years.
 ¥ Now, whenever we travel somewhere, museums are usually at the top of his list.
 ¥ He can spend hours, or sometimes even an entire day, walking through a museum and
 reading almost every piece of information on display.
 ¥ The rest of us are tired and ready to leave long before he is!
 ¥ He also learns history by watching documentaries, reading articles, and searching for
 information online.
 ¥ He is especially interested in wars and major events that changed the world.
 ¥ He wants to understand why wars started, what decisions were made, and how they
 affected different countries and ordinary people.
 ¥ I think he loves history now because he has realized that it is not just about memorizing
 dates and names.
 ¥ He enjoys connecting past events with the modern world and understanding why different
 countries and cultures are the way they are.
 ¥ I really admire this quality in my dad.
 ¥ I find it fascinating that someone who once thought history was boring can now be so
 engrossed in it.
 ¥ He makes our family trips more interesting because he shares interesting facts about the
 places we visit.
 ¥ Sometimes we have to drag him out of museums, but I think his curiosity and desire to keep
 learning are qualities that I really respect.

PART 3 - FOLLOW-UP QUESTIONS

Besides big historical events, what else is important when learning history?
Besides historical events, I think it is important to learn how ordinary people lived in the past. This
gives us a better understanding of what society was like rather than just learning about political
events, rulers, and wars.

Do you think people should learn ancient history before learning modern history?
I don't think it is necessary to learn about ancient history before modern history. People can start
with modern history as it is easier to relate to events that happened more recently. Once they
understand modern events, they can become curious about the past and learn how earlier events
influenced them.

Is it important to learn how people in the past lived their lives?
Yes, learning about people's daily lives helps us understand how society has changed over time. For
example, we can compare their transportaEon, jobs, living condiEons, and educaEon with what we
have today. It can also make us appreciate some of the comforts and opportuniEes we have
nowadays.

How do children learn history before they attend school?
Children can learn about history from their parents and grandparents through family stories,
tradiEons, and various fesEvals that people celebrate at their homes. They can also visit museums,
historical monuments, and other such places with their families. Children can also learn about the
past through books, movies, cartoons, etc.

At what age do you think people should begin to learn history?
I think children can start learning about basic history from the age of six or seven. I think history can
be taught through pictures, stories, and interesEng acEviEes like visiEng a museum rather than just
memorizing dates and events. As they get older, they can learn about the complex events and their
causes and consequences

Do you think children should begin learning history from an early age?
Yes, Children should be introduced to history from an early age because it will help them
understand where they came from. They will know more about their culture and tradiEons. It also
makes them curious about other countries and civilizaEons. I think history should be taught in an
interesEng way rather than have children just memorize events and dates

What are the differences between young people's leisure time activities and those of old people?
Young people like to hang out with their friends in their leisure time. Some like to watch movies and
TV shows on the OTT. Some like to play video games, and some like to spend time on social media.
Older people spend their leisure time in parks and open areas where they can just sit around or talk
to other people. They also spend their time reading newspapers and watching TV.

What can we do to bring family members closer together?
Family members can become closer by spending quality time together. For example, they can have
meals together, celebrate special occasions, or simply talk about their day. They can also go on
vacations together, where they get a break from daily responsibilities and just enjoy each other's
company. Also, their bonds can get stronger if they support each other during difficult times.

How do people get along in a big family?
I think most people in a big family can get along if they are patient, understanding, and respectful of
each other. They can maintain good relationships if they communicate openly and support one
another. Since people can have different opinions and personalities, sometimes accepting them as
they are, is the best way to live happily together as a family.

 ¥ In fact, there is only a three-day difference between our ages, so we have grown up together

 ¥ She studied computer engineering and later moved to the USA, where she completed her

 ¥ She is also very good at explaining the process, so she conducts crochet workshops and online
 classes for people who want to learn it.
 ¥ Many people have learnt crocheting from her because she explains each step slowly and
 makes it easy to follow.
 ¥ She has become so good at it that she receives orders from customers not only in India but
 also from other countries.
 ¥ I think she is good at making things by hand because she is patient, creative and pays
 attention to small details.
 ¥ For me, it is quite impressive that she learnt everything on her own and turned a simple
 hobby into something she can teach and create beautiful things with.

PART 3 - FOLLOW-UP QUESTIONS
1. Does art, like music or theater, have an impact on society?
Yes, I think art has a strong impact on society because it can influence the way people think and
feel. Music and theater can also bring people together and spread important messages. For
example, a good movie or play can make people think about social problems that they normally
ignore.

2. Is it good to have creative people in a team?
Definitely, because creative people often come up with ideas that others may not think of. They can
help a team find different solutions when there is a problem. For example, in a college project, one
creative person can completely change the way the whole team presents its work.

3. What impact can artistic buildings have on society?
In my opinion, artistic buildings can make a city more attractive and give it a different identity. They
can also become popular tourist places and create business for the local area. At the same time, I
think they should be practical as well, because a beautiful building is not very useful if people
cannot use it comfortably.

4. Do children these days still like doing arts and crafts?
Well, some children definitely do, but I think they have more distractions than children had in the
past. Phones, games and videos take up a lot of their free time now. However, if parents or
teachers give them simple materials and encourage them, many children still enjoy making things
with their hands.

5. Why do some children enjoy making things by hand?
I believe children enjoy it because they can make something with their own hands and immediately
see the result. It also gives them a chance to use their imagination instead of simply following
instructions. For example, a child may enjoy making a paper house even if it looks very simple.

6. Do you think art classes are important for children?
Yes, I think they are important because they help children become more creative and confident.
Not every child is good at academic subjects, so art gives them another way to express themselves.
Also, when children make something themselves and receive encouragement, it can give them a
real sense of achievement

 18. Describe a time when someone talked about
 something you were not interested in but you kept
 listening
You should say:
 Who the person was
 What he/she talked about
 Why you kept listening
 And explain how you felt about the experience

 ¥ I remember an incident that happened quite recently, just after my 12th standard exams
 were over.
 ¥ I had a few weeks free, and honestly, I was getting quite bored sitting at home.
 ¥ I had been watching a lot of travel vlogs, so I decided to use my vacation to visit a hill
 station.
 ¥ The weather in my area was very hot because I live in the plains, so I really wanted to go
 somewhere cooler.
 ¥ I asked my parents for permission, and surprisingly, they happily agreed because I was
 going to stay with one of my relatives.
 ¥ I booked an early morning bus to a nearby hill station, and it was actually my first solo
 journey.
 ¥ I reached the bus stand at around 4 a.m., and the journey was going to take almost eight
 hours.
 ¥ I was very tired because I had hardly slept the previous night, so I was hoping to sleep
 during the journey.
 ¥ However, an old man sitting next to me started talking to me as soon as the bus left.
 ¥ He first asked me some normal questions, like where I was from, what I had studied and
 where I was going.
 ¥ I answered him politely, but then he started telling me about his life and the different jobs
 he had done.
 ¥ I actually enjoyed listening to that part because he had some interesting experiences and I
 was curious about his life.
 ¥ But after some time, the conversation moved towards politics, which is something I am
 not really interested in.
 ¥ He started giving his opinions about the government, the country and what people should
 do, and he was also quite critical of almost everyone.
 ¥ I did not really agree with some of his views, and I honestly wanted to change the topic,
 but I did not want to be rude to him.
 ¥ I also thought about changing my seat, but the bus was completely full, so that was not
 really an option.
 ¥ I kept listening for some time because he was an elderly person and I felt it would be
 disrespectful to completely ignore him.
 ¥ Finally, when the bus stopped for snacks and refreshments, I had something to eat, and
 after getting back on the bus, I put on my headphones and slept for most of the remaining
 journey.

 ¥ I felt a little bored during that conversation, but at the same time, I realised that
 sometimes we have to listen to people even when their interests are completely different
 from ours.

PART 3 - FOLLOW-UP QUESTIONS

1. How can we tell if a listener is listening carefully?
I think their body language gives us a good idea. They usually maintain eye contact, nod their head
and respond at the right time. For example, when I talk to my friends, I can easily tell if they are
listening because they remember small details from what I said.

2. What would happen if the listener didn't listen carefully?
Well, it can easily lead to misunderstandings or even arguments. The person may miss important
information and later ask the same questions again. For example, if a student doesn't listen
carefully in class, they may struggle to complete the homework because they missed the teacher's
instructions.

3. What kind of topics do young people like to talk about today?
According to me, young people mostly talk about entertainment, social media, sports, travel and
their studies or careers. They also discuss new trends, movies, music and things they see online. Of
course, it depends on the person, but I think social media has made it easier for young people to
find common topics to talk about.

 ¥ The person I want to talk about is my cousin Jagdeep, who works in a government job and

 ¥ I first noticed his passion through the pictures he used to share in our family groups and
 on his social media.
 ¥ His interest actually started with wildlife photography, especially pictures of birds.
 ¥ He has photographed many different Indian birds, and some of his pictures are really

 ¥ I once travelled with him to the forest area behind Sukhna Lake in Chandigarh, and that

 ¥ We went there early in the morning because that is usually a good time to see birds.

 ¥ He has invested a lot of money in his equipment, probably more than 10 to 15 lakh rupees

 ¥ He even has a special refrigerator at home to keep his camera lenses safe from moisture,

 ¥ What impressed me even more was his knowledge of birds.
 ¥ While I could only see green trees and leaves, he could hear a sound and tell which bird
 was nearby.
 ¥ His children are also quite familiar with birds because they have grown up seeing him do
 this.
 ¥ He travels to different parts of India, especially bird sanctuaries and natural areas, and he
 always carries his camera gear with him.
 ¥ I also remember staying at his home once and seeing him put some breadcrumbs on his
 balcony so that birds would come closer and he could photograph them.
 ¥ He has also created a small photography community and regularly shares his pictures
 online with a short motivational caption.
 ¥ In fact, I don't think there has been a single day in the last three or four years when he has
 missed posting a picture.
 ¥ Recently, he has also started exploring macro photography, so now he takes close-up
 pictures of small objects and other subjects.
 ¥ I really admire him because he learnt most of this on his own, and he has stayed
 disciplined and consistent with it for so many years.
 ¥ He is also a very polite and soft-spoken person, so I think his personality matches his
 hobby quite well.
 ¥ What I have learnt from him is that if you stay interested in something, practise regularly
 and don't give up, you can become really good at it over time.

PART 3 - FOLLOW-UP QUESTIONS
1. When do people like taking photos?
People usually like taking photos when they are doing something special or visiting a new place.
Birthdays, weddings, holidays and trips are common examples. Personally, I take more photos when
I travel because I want to remember the places I visit.

2. Some people don't mind others walking into their shot when they're taking photos. What do you
think?
I think it depends on the type of photo. If it is just a casual picture, I don't think it is a big problem,
but if someone is trying to take an important photo, others should try not to walk in front of them.
Well, a little consideration can save a lot of frustration.

3. What are the downsides of constantly taking photos while traveling?
According to me, the biggest problem is that people can become more focused on their phones
than on the actual place. They may spend several minutes trying to get the perfect picture instead
of enjoying the experience. For example, someone might visit a beautiful viewpoint but remember
the photos rather than the actual feeling of being there.

4. Do you think it's ok to take photos everywhere?
No, not everywhere. I think people should respect privacy and follow the rules of the place,
especially in museums, religious places or private areas. Taking a photo may seem harmless to one
person, but it could make someone else uncomfortable.

5. What kind of photos do people often take?
I think people mostly take photos of themselves, their friends and family, especially when they are
travelling or attending special events. Food, scenery and pets are also very common subjects. These
days, people also take lots of short photos and videos for social media.

6. Why are some people keen on taking photos?
I believe many people take photos because they want to keep memories of important moments.
Some people also genuinely enjoy photography and like improving their skills. For others, sharing
good pictures online is another reason, because they enjoy getting reactions from their friends.

 20. Describe a new skill you learned when you were
 a child

 What the skill was
 Who taught you this skill
 How you learned it
 And explain how you felt about learning the skill

¥ One skill I learnt when I was a child was dance, and I actually learnt different types of dance,
 including Bhangra, Bollywood and Western dance.
¥ I was around nine or ten years old at that time, and my summer vacations were going on.
¥ I was getting bored at home because I had a lot of free time and didn't have much to do.
¥ One day, I saw an advertisement in the newspaper about a new dance academy that had
 opened near my house.
¥ They were also offering a discount for the first hundred students, so I showed the advertisement
 to my parents.
¥ They liked the idea and took me there to see the academy and meet the trainer.
¥ The trainer was an experienced dancer, and he was quite famous because he had actually been
 a contestant on Dance India Dance, which was a very popular TV show.
¥ He was very friendly with children and did not make the classes feel like serious training.
¥ He would first demonstrate a step himself and then break it down into small parts so that we
 could learn it easily.
¥ I joined the academy for about one month, and honestly, it was one of the best experiences of
 my childhood.
¥ The class was one hour long, but it was quite exhausting because we were constantly moving,
 and by the end, our clothes were often completely drenched in sweat.
¥ In the beginning, I had almost no flexibility, and I didn't even know some of the basic dance
 moves.
¥ So I used to struggle a lot, but I slowly started getting better with regular practice.
¥ After a month, I could do many different steps and even perform on several Bollywood songs.
¥ I also practised at home after my classes by playing songs on the TV and trying to copy and
 repeat the steps I had learnt.
¥ Apart from dancing, I also started doing some basic stretching exercises because I wanted to
 become more flexible.
¥ Later, this skill became very useful because I got opportunities to perform in school dance
 competitions and family functions.
¥ It also helped me make new friends, and I became much more confident about performing in
 front of other people.
¥ Looking back, I think the biggest lesson I learnt was that practice is probably the most important
 part of learning any skill.
¥ Even though I only learnt dance for a month at the academy, that experience stayed with me
 and helped me become more confident and active as a person.

PART 3 - FOLLOW-UP QUESTIONS
1. Is it important for everyone to have a specialized skill?
I think it is useful for people to have at least one skill that they are really good at. It can help them
in their studies, career or even daily life. However, I don't think everyone needs to become an
expert because having a few basic skills can also be enough for some people.

2. Can a child learn skills from another child?
Yes, definitely. Children often learn very quickly by watching and copying other children, and they
may feel more comfortable learning from someone their own age. For example, a child who is good
at drawing can easily teach another child some simple drawing techniques.

3. Do you think it is easier for children to learn new skills than for adults?
Generally, yes, because children are usually more open to trying new things and they don't worry
too much about making mistakes. They also have fewer responsibilities, so they can spend more
time practising. Adults can learn just as well, but they may have less time and more fear of failure.

4. What are the differences between the way adults learn and the way children learn?
Children usually learn through playing, copying others and trying things themselves. Adults tend to
be more focused and want to understand why something works before they learn it. For example, a
child may learn cycling simply by trying again and again, while an adult might first look for
instructions or watch a video.

5. What are the differences between learning in a group and learning by yourself?
Well, learning in a group can be more enjoyable because you can discuss things and learn from
other people's mistakes. Learning alone gives you more freedom because you can go at your own
speed. Personally, I think a combination of both is best, depending on the skill you are trying to
learn.

6. What skills should children learn before they go to school?
In my opinion, children should learn some basic communication and social skills before starting
school. They should know how to share, listen to others, follow simple instructions and take care of
their own things. Basic skills like eating independently and keeping their belongings in place can
also make the transition to school much easier.

 What the special thing was
 Why you wanted to buy it
 How you saved money for it
 And explain how you felt after buying it

 ¥ I would like to talk about a time when I saved money to buy something quite special for
 myself, which was an iPhone 17 Pro.
 ¥ My old phone was an Android phone, and I had been using it for more than five years.
 ¥ It had become very slow and would hang quite often, especially when I was using several apps
 at the same time.
 ¥ I had always wanted to experience an iPhone, but for a long time, it was simply out of my
 budget.
 ¥ So, I decided that instead of asking my parents to buy it for me, I would try to save the money
 myself.
 ¥ I started putting aside a part of my pocket money every month and kept it in a separate place.
 ¥ For a few months, I also stopped eating outside and avoided buying new clothes unless I really
 needed them.
 ¥ Sometimes, when I needed some extra money, I asked my parents for a little more pocket
 money and saved that as well.
 ¥ I also tried to find a part-time job so that I could increase my savings and reach my target a
 little faster.
 ¥ It was not always easy because there were times when I wanted to spend the money on other
 things.
 ¥ But whenever I felt like spending it, I reminded myself that I was saving for something I really
 wanted.
 ¥ I kept doing this for several months, and slowly the amount in my savings started becoming
 bigger.
 ¥ Finally, just before my birthday, I realised that I had saved enough money to buy the phone.
 ¥ I decided to treat it as a birthday gift to myself, which made the purchase feel even more
 special.
 ¥ I was really excited when I finally bought it because it was something I had worked and saved
 for myself.
 ¥ I especially love its camera quality, as I enjoy taking photographs, and the pictures look much
 better than those from my old phone.
 ¥ I also like the smooth performance, the display and the overall features because everything
 feels much faster and easier to use.
 ¥ More importantly, I don't want to keep changing phones every few years, so I plan to use this
 phone for at least six or seven years.
 ¥ Looking back, I think saving for it made me appreciate the phone more because I knew exactly
 how much effort had gone into buying it.
 ¥ It also taught me that when you really want something, saving a little regularly and controlling
 unnecessary spending can help you reach your goal

PART 3 - FOLLOW-UP QUESTIONS

1. Should people spend time on charity, or just money?
According to me, both are useful, but time can sometimes be more valuable than money. People
 can volunteer, teach children or help older people, and these things need personal effort. Of
 course, donating money is much easier for busy people, so I think it depends on what a person
 can afford to give.

2. What kind of things do people usually save money for?
People usually save for things that are expensive, such as a house, car, education or a holiday. Some
 also keep money aside for emergencies because unexpected expenses can come up at any time.
 Personally, I think saving for a specific goal makes it easier because you know exactly what you
 are working towards.

3. Is it easier to spend money or save money?
Well, spending money is definitely easier because there are so many things available to buy,
 especially online. Saving requires self-control, as you have to say no to some things you want. For
 example, if I get some extra money, I may want to spend it immediately, but having a saving goal
 makes it easier to control myself.

4. Do you think it is easier to teach teenagers how to manage money when they are around 16 or
 17 years old?
Yes, I think that is a good age because teenagers are becoming more independent and starting to
 make their own choices. They can learn simple things like making a budget, saving part of their
 pocket money and avoiding unnecessary spending. If they learn these habits early, managing
 money as adults should become easier.

5. Should parents teach their children to spend money freely or to save money?
I believe parents should teach children to do both, but saving should be given more importance.
 Children should enjoy their money sometimes, otherwise they may see saving as a punishment.
 At the same time, parents can give them a small amount of money and let them decide how
 much to spend and how much to keep.

6. Should schools teach children how to manage money from a young age?
Definitely, because money management is a basic life skill that every child will need later. Schools
 could teach simple things like budgeting, saving, banking and understanding the difference
 between needs and wants. I think even a few practical lessons can help children make better
 financial decisions when they grow up.18. Describe a natural place in your city that you enjoy
 visiting

 ¥ I normally go there on Sunday mornings, mostly with my friends, and sometimes I take my

 that I need some peace and quiet.
 ¥ Overall, I enjoy going there because it gives me a chance to slow down, spend time in nature
 and come back feeling fresh.

PART 3 - FOLLOW-UP QUESTIONS
1. Which is more important, protecting the environment or developing the economy?
In my opinion, both are important, but protecting the environment should not be ignored just for
economic growth. A strong economy is useful, but if pollution and climate problems become too
serious, people will suffer in the long run. I think governments should try to find a balance, for
example by supporting businesses that create jobs without causing too much damage to nature.

2. Do you think staying in a natural place would help change people's mood?
Yes, definitely. Natural places are usually quieter and less stressful than busy cities, so spending
some time there can help people relax and clear their minds. Personally, I feel much better after
walking in a forest or spending time near a lake, especially when I have had a busy week.

3. Do you think there should be many natural places in cities?
Yes, I strongly believe cities should have more parks, forests and green spaces. They give people a
place to exercise, relax and spend time away from traffic and buildings. Also, these places can
improve the quality of life in a city, so I think they should be considered an important part of city
planning.

 organized

 Who this person is
 What this person usually does to stay organized
 In what situations you have noticed this quality
 And explain why you think being organized is important to him/her

¥ The person I want to talk about is my mother, who is probably the most organised person I
 know.
¥ She recently retired as a principal of a government school, where she taught Economics before
 becoming a principal.
¥ Even after retirement, she has not retired from organising things at home.
¥ In fact, I sometimes feel she is almost obsessed with keeping everything in its proper place.
¥ She would probably have a nightmare if she saw things lying around randomly.
¥ I see this every single day at home, because she has a place for almost everything.
¥ For example, as soon as we come home, our shoes have to go straight to the shoe rack, and our
 car and house keys have to be put on the key hanger.
¥ If we wash our clothes and put them out to dry, they are folded and put into the wardrobe as
 soon as they are dry.
¥ Even making the bed is part of her morning routine, and it is usually the first thing she does after
 getting up.
¥ Sometimes, if we leave something on the dining table or another table, we may not find it there
 five minutes later.
¥ My mother has already picked it up and put it somewhere she thinks is safe and proper.
¥ The funny part is that sometimes she puts something away so carefully that even she forgets
 where she kept it.
¥ Then all of us start looking around the house and asking each other, "Where would Mum have
 kept it?"
¥ When we finally ask her, she usually says, "I was just taking care of it," which makes us laugh.
¥ She doesn't only organise things; she also likes to plan her time, household work and daily tasks
 properly.
¥ I think being organised is important to her because she likes the house to look clean, tidy and
 peaceful.
¥ She also believes that when everything is in the right place, you don't waste time looking for
 things.
¥ Sometimes her habit can be a little annoying for the rest of us, but honestly, I think it keeps our
 home running smoothly.
¥ I really love my mother because behind all this organising, she is simply trying to take care of
 everyone and make our lives easier.
¥ So, although we sometimes joke about her habit, I really admire her discipline and the way she
 manages everything.

PART 3 - FOLLOW-UP QUESTIONS
1. Do you think it is important for managers to be well organized?
Yes, definitely. Managers have to handle many tasks and people, so being organized helps them
manage everything properly. It also saves time and reduces mistakes. For example, an organized
manager can plan meetings and deadlines much more effectively.

2. Should parents teach their children to be organized?
Yes, I think they should. Children can learn simple habits like keeping their books, toys and clothes
in the right place. These habits can make them more responsible and independent as they grow up.
I think parents should teach this through daily routines.

3. What can people do to become more organized?
People can start by making a simple daily schedule and writing down important tasks. They should
also keep their belongings in fixed places and avoid delaying work. For example, making a to-do list
every morning can help a person stay focused.

4. Are organized people more likely to be successful at work or in their studies?
Yes, in many cases they are. Organized people usually manage their time better and are less likely
to forget important tasks or deadlines. However, organization alone cannot guarantee success
because skills and hard work are also important.

5. Do you think children should learn to be organized from a young age?
Yes, definitely. Young children can easily develop good habits if they are taught early. They can
learn to keep their school bag ready, arrange their toys and finish homework on time. These small
habits can help them later in life.

6. Why is it important for people to be organized?
Being organized helps people save time and complete their work without unnecessary stress. It also
makes it easier to remember important things and manage different responsibilities. In my opinion,
an organized person can have a more balanced and less stressful life.

 24. Describe a time you watched a famous person
 being interviewed
You should say:
 Who the famous person was
 What the interview was about
 When and where you watched the interview
 And explain how you felt about the interview

 ¥ The famous person I want to talk about is Bhagwant Mann, the Chief Minister of Punjab.
 ¥ A couple of months ago, I watched an interview of him with a well-known news anchor on
 India Today.
 ¥ I actually watched it on YouTube because one of my friends shared the video with me.
 ¥ At that time, my college exams had just finished, so I had quite a lot of free time.
 ¥ I was mostly playing games, watching videos and just relaxing, so I decided to watch the
 interview.
 ¥ The interview was mainly about the performance of his government during his time as Chief
 Minister.
 ¥ What immediately caught my attention was the way he answered the questions.
 ¥ Bhagwant Mann was a comedian before entering politics, so he is very quick with words and
 often adds humour to his replies.
 ¥ There was one particular moment that I found really interesting when the anchor asked him
 about some of his party members joining the opposition.
 ¥ Instead of simply answering the question, Mann turned it around and asked the anchor about
 his own career.
 ¥ He pointed out that the anchor had also worked for a different media organisation in the past
 and had later moved to another one.
 ¥ The anchor seemed a little surprised by the reply, and for a moment, he didn't really have a
 strong counter to it.
 ¥ I found that exchange quite entertaining because it felt spontaneous rather than rehearsed.
 ¥ I have watched many political interviews before, but some of them can feel very serious or
 even scripted.
 ¥ This one felt more natural because Mann was able to use jokes, quick replies and even his
 sense of humour during the conversation.
 ¥ I also find it interesting that he can make poems and jokes on the spot, which probably comes
 from his background as a performer.
 ¥ At the same time, the interview also discussed areas such as education and healthcare and
 what his government was trying to do in Punjab.
 ¥ Overall, I really enjoyed watching it because it was informative but also entertaining, and I
 never felt like switching it off.
 ¥ It also reminded me that a good interview is not only about the questions; the personality of
 the person being interviewed can make the whole conversation much more interesting.

PART 3 - FOLLOW-UP QUESTIONS
1. What should people wear to a job interview?
I think people should wear clean, formal and well-fitting clothes. They should avoid wearing
anything too casual or flashy. For example, a shirt with trousers or a formal suit can create a good
first impression.

2. Why do we need to attend interviews when applying for jobs?
Interviews help employers understand a candidate better than just looking at their CV. They can
check their communication skills, confidence and attitude. It also gives the candidate a chance to
learn more about the company and the job.

3. What should people do to prepare for a job interview?
People should research the company and understand the job requirements before the interview.
They should also prepare answers to common questions and practise speaking clearly. I think doing
a mock interview with a friend can also be very helpful.

4. What can we do to get a job interview?
First, we need a good CV that clearly shows our education, skills and experience. We can apply
through company websites, job portals or professional contacts. Having the right qualifications and
writing a good application can increase our chances of getting an interview.

5. What kinds of things do famous people often say in an interview?
Famous people often talk about their career, achievements and future plans. They may also discuss
their personal experiences, challenges and opinions about their work. Sometimes, interviewers ask
them about rumours or controversial topics as well.

6. What kinds of people are frequently interviewed?
Celebrities, politicians, athletes, business leaders and experts are frequently interviewed. They
usually have something interesting to share with the public because of their work or experience.
During major events, even ordinary people may be interviewed if they have witnessed something
important.

PART 3 - FOLLOW-UP QUESTIONS
1. Do you think doing a job that you like is more important than other things?
Yes, I think it is very important because people spend a large part of their lives at work. If they enjoy
 their job, they are more likely to feel satisfied and motivated. However, salary and job security are
 also important, so people need to find a good balance.

2. What would make a student happy at school?
I think friendly teachers, good friends and an enjoyable learning environment can make students
 happy. They also feel good when they understand a difficult topic or get good marks. For example,
 praise from a teacher can really motivate a student.

3. When do you think people are happiest?
I think people are happiest when they are spending time with people they love and doing things they
 enjoy. Achieving an important goal can also bring a lot of happiness. For example, a student may
 feel extremely happy after getting good results in an important exam.

4. How can the arts spread positive emotions in society?
Art, music, movies and theatre can make people feel relaxed and happy. They can also bring people
 together because people enjoy sharing the same songs, performances or cultural activities. For
 example, a concert can create a very positive atmosphere among thousands of people.

5. Do you think teachers are able to spot the unhappy children?
Yes, experienced teachers can often notice changes in a child's behaviour. An unhappy child may
 become unusually quiet, stop participating in class or avoid other students. However, teachers
 may not always understand the exact reason, so they should talk to the child privately and gently.

 6. Should teachers know what children are doing all the time?
I don't think teachers need to know what children are doing all the time. They should keep an eye on
 students during school hours, especially to make sure they are safe and behaving properly.
 However, children also need some privacy and freedom to make their own choices. I think teachers
 should step in when a child's behaviour becomes harmful or affects others.

with others. They may also be good at sports, studies or other activities. Sometimes, students
become popular simply because they have a fun personality and make others feel comfortable.

create pressure to impress others. Some popular students may spend too much time trying to
maintain their image instead of focusing on their studies. So, I think it has both advantages and
disadvantages.

PART 3 - FOLLOW-UP QUESTIONS
1. Which is better, to make a decision quickly or slowly?
I think it depends on the situation. Small decisions can be made quickly, but important decisions
should be considered carefully. Taking some time helps us understand the possible results and
avoid making mistakes.

2. Do you think most of people's decisions are made in their 20s?
Yes, I think many important decisions are made in the 20s. People often choose their career, higher
education and sometimes their life partner during this period. However, people can change their
decisions later because their priorities and situations can change.

3. What if people in a group have different opinions on a decision?
I think they should discuss their opinions calmly and listen to each other. They can compare the
advantages and disadvantages of each idea and then choose the option that works best for
everyone. If they still cannot agree, they can take a vote.

4. What little decisions do people make every day?
People make many small decisions, such as what to eat, what to wear, when to exercise and how to
spend their free time. At work or school, they also decide which tasks to do first. Most of these
decisions do not require much thought.

5. Do you think it is a good idea to make a small decision every day?
Yes, I think it can be useful. Making small decisions independently can improve a person's
confidence and decision-making skills. For example, deciding what task to finish first each day can
help someone become more responsible.

6. How can we tell the difference between an important and an easy decision?
An important decision usually has a bigger effect on our future and may be difficult to change later.
An easy decision normally has fewer consequences and can be changed easily. For example,
choosing a career is important, while choosing what to eat for lunch is usually an easy decision.

PART 3 - FOLLOW-UP QUESTIONS

1. In which sectors are companies more likely to succeed in India?
I think companies have good opportunities in technology, healthcare, education, manufacturing,
and e-commerce. India has a huge population, so there is a large potential market for businesses.
For example, technology companies can grow quickly because more and more people are using
smartphones and online services. I also think healthcare and education will continue to grow
because they are essential parts of people's lives.

2. Is it easy to start up a company?
No, I don't think it is particularly easy. A person needs enough capital, a good business idea, and a
proper understanding of the market. Also, there is also a lot of corruption and bureaucracy that can
make it difficult to start businesses early, but things are slowly changing for the better. Financially,
also governments are providing a lot of support in the form of funds and guidance to startups.

3. Other than making money, what is more important for a company?
I think customer satisfaction and a good reputation are extremely important. If a company provides
good-quality products or services, customers are more likely to trust it and recommend it to others.
Moreover, no company can grow without a healthy society, so I think every company also needs to
do something for society as well.

4. Does money equal success?
Not necessarily. Money is certainly one measure of success, but I don't think it is the only one. For
example, someone may earn a lot of money but have no free time or personal satisfaction. On the
other hand, a person who has a modest income but enjoys their job and has a happy family life
could also be considered successful. So, I think success depends on a person's goals and priorities.

5. How can companies help protect the environment?
Companies can take several steps to reduce their environmental impact. They can reduce plastic
and paper waste, recycle materials, and reduce unnecessary packaging. They can also encourage
employees to use public transport or work remotely when possible. Larger companies can make an
even bigger difference by switching to renewable energy like solar panels.

6. Is it difficult to do business in today's world?
Yes, I think it is quite difficult because competition is extremely high. Companies have to keep up
with changing technology and constantly changing customer expectations. Social media has also
made businesses more visible, but on the downside a single negative review can sometimes
damage a company's reputation. At the same time, there are many opportunities because
companies can reach customers around the world through the internet. So, I would say that doing
business is challenging, but there are also more opportunities than in the past.

PART 3 - FOLLOW-UP QUESTIONS

1. Why do people want to be popular?
I think people want to be popular because they enjoy attention and recognition from others. Being
popular can also make people feel more confident and respected. For some people, it can provide
career opportunities, especially if they work in entertainment, sports, or social media. However, I
think some people simply want to feel accepted and valued by others.

2. Is it easier to become popular nowadays?
Yes, definitely. Social media has made it much easier for ordinary people to become popular. In the
past, people usually needed to appear on television or in newspapers to become famous, but
nowadays, a person can become well-known by creating interesting content on platforms such as
Instagram or YouTube. However, becoming popular is easy, but staying popular for a long time is
much more difficult.

3. What are the qualities of being a good teacher?
I think a good teacher should be knowledgeable, patient, supportive, and good at communication.
They should be able to explain difficult ideas in a simple way and understand that students learn at
different speeds. I also think a good teacher should encourage students rather than simply criticise
them when they make mistakes.

4. Do you think good teachers are always popular among students?
Not necessarily. A good teacher may sometimes be strict or demanding, so students may not like
them very much. However, that doesn't mean they are not effective teachers. For example, a
teacher who gives regular homework and maintains discipline might seem unpopular at first, but
students may appreciate them later because they help them achieve better results.

5. Is it important for a teacher to be popular?
I don't think popularity is essential. A teacher's main responsibility is to educate students, not to
make them like them. Of course, having a friendly relationship with students can make learning
more enjoyable, but a teacher also needs to maintain discipline and set clear expectations. So, I
would say being respected is more important than being popular.

6. Why are some students popular in school?
There are several reasons. Some students are popular because they are friendly, confident, and
good at communicating with others. Others may be talented at sports, music, or other activities,
which naturally attracts attention. In some cases, students become popular simply because they
have a large group of friends or are active on social media. I think personality usually plays the
biggest role.
